Chapter VIII · Unit 138

The choice of the twists: Galois representations of lifts, Selmer groups made short by one anticyclotomic twist, and congruence bounds by Ribet's method

note and structured proofs · 205 declarations · 96 structured proofs · 3 notes

For admissible data D and every prime \ell with a fixed isomorphism \mathbb{C} \simeq \overline{\mathbb{Q}}_\ell, the unit chooses weight-zero anticyclotomic twists \vartheta for which the congruence bounds of a fit family hold (exists_isFitFamily): the \ell-adic Galois representations attached to the forms of the auxiliary pairs are read as columns and rows (conjugate self-dual, with prescribed Hodge–Tate jumps, labelled by the tame characters at the chosen places), one anticyclotomic twist with tied auxiliary primes makes their Selmer groups short, and Ribet's method with congruent blocks gives a Hecke element whose valuation at the lift is within the budget, the crystalline losses above \ell being affordable. Chunk 138-a holds the definitions (columns, rows, apt label tuples, the Selmer length bound, families of interpolated L-values, pencils of tied primes, balanced representations, even classes, blocks and the cocycles that obey relations), the existence of anticyclotomic characters at new primes, the Galois representations of labelled columns, and the crystalline defect at one place above \ell.

Notes

  • Columns and rows as Galois representations: labels at the chosen places, anticyclotomic characters at new primes, families of L-values and the crystalline loss at one place 43 pages · PDF

  • structureIsConjSelfDuallines 21–24

    For admissible data D, a prime \ell, a finite type n, a natural number t and an \ell-adic representation R : \operatorname{Gal}(\Omega /E) \to \operatorname{GL}_n(\overline{\mathbb{Q}}_\ell ): IsConjSelfDual t R holds when (continuous) R is continuous; (nondeg) the trace form is nondegenerate on the span of the R(g) (IsTraceNondeg R); and (trace_conj) for every g \in \operatorname{Gal}(\Omega /E), \operatorname{tr} R(g^c) = cycInv(g)^{t-1}\cdot \operatorname{tr} R(g^{-1}), cycInv the inverse \ell-adic cyclotomic character and t - 1 truncated at 0 (so t = 0 reads as t = 1).

  • defcrudeUnitline 27

    For a natural number \ell and admissible data D: crudeUnit \ell D = \operatorname{cr}_\ell = v_\ell (Mk) + 2^{-\ell }, a nonnegative real number, where Mk = D.chosenGroupOrder is the product over the chosen places w of E of the orders of \operatorname{GL}_{rt}(\mathbb{F}_w), and v_\ell = padicValNat \ell.

  • defSelmerLengthLelines 34–38

    For admissible data D, a prime \ell, a finite type n, a character \psi : \operatorname{Gal}(\Omega /E) \to \overline{\mathbb{Q}}_\ell, an \ell-adic representation R : \operatorname{Gal}(\Omega /E) \to \operatorname{GL}_n(\overline{\mathbb{Q}}_\ell ), a set Q of finite places of E, m \in \mathbb{N}, \gamma \in \operatorname{Gal}(\Omega /E) and z in the integers of \overline{\mathbb{Q}}_\ell: SelmerLengthLe \psi R Q m \gamma z holds when for all sufficiently large coefficient rings O (eventually along the order of CoeffRing \ell) there are a lattice u : \operatorname{Gal}(\Omega /E) \to M_n(O) of R (IsLatticeIn R u), a character \psi ' : \operatorname{Gal}(\Omega /E) \to O with \psi '(g) = \psi (g) for all g, and y \in O equal to z, such that, for \rho = \psi '\cdot u (twistRep (\operatorname{id}) \psi ' u): \rho (\gamma ) = y\cdot 1, y - 1 is a unit of O, \operatorname{cyc}(\gamma ) = 1, and the normalised O-length of the cocycles vanishing at \gamma in the Selmer module of \rho modulo \ell ^m (locally constant cocycles \operatorname{Gal}(\Omega /E) \to (O/\ell ^m)^n, unramified outside the places above \ell, above g_0 or g_1, and in Q or its conjugate, obeying crysRels \rho) is at most 10\cdot f\cdot |n|\cdot \operatorname{cr}_\ell + \operatorname{cr}_\ell , f = D.f the number of infinite places of E and \operatorname{cr}_\ell = crudeUnit \ell D.

  • defobeyingCocycleslines 16–17

    Let O be a commutative ring, G a group, \sigma and d finite types, X a type, u = (u_x)_{x\in X} a family of homomorphisms u_x : G \to M_\sigma (O), \rho : G \to M_d(O) a homomorphism and a \in O. obeyingCocycles u \rho a is the O-submodule of the functions c : G \to (O/aO)^d (no continuity asked) such that (1) c is a cocycle for \rho: c(gh) = c(g) + \rho (g)\cdot c(h) for all g, h \in G (the relations cocycleRels \rho), and (2) \sum _g \theta (g)\cdot c(g) = 0 for every finitely supported \theta : G \to O with \sum _g \theta (g)\cdot (u_x(g) - 1) = 0 in M_\sigma (O) for every x \in X.

    Definition 2.1, p. 6 of A fit family of twists exists: Ribet's method with tied primes, triangular Taylor–Wiles primes and the denominator of the projection to theta lifts Definition 3.1, p. 8 of Columns and rows as Galois representations: labels at the chosen places, anticyclotomic characters at new primes, families of L-values and the crystalline loss at one place Definition 2.1, p. 5 of Short Selmer groups after one anticyclotomic twist, balanced rows, and Ribet's lemma to first order

    The Lean statement

  • lemmaobeyingCocycles.exists_graph_subrepresentationlines 22–37argument

    Let O be a commutative Noetherian ring, G a group, X a finite type, \sigma and d finite types, u = (u_x)_{x\in X} homomorphisms u_x : G \to M_\sigma (O), \rho : G \to M_d(O) a homomorphism, a \in O, and c \in obeyingCocycles u \rho a. Let G act O-linearly on M = (M_\sigma (O))^X by g\cdot (m_x)_x = (u_x(g)m_x)_x and on (O/aO)^d through \rho. Then there are k \in \mathbb{N} and a G-stable O-submodule Y of M \times (O/aO)^d (a subrepresentation of the product representation) such that the first projection Y \to M is injective and, for all g \in G and v \in M, ((u_x(g) - 1)_x + a^k\cdot v, c(g)) \in Y. So c is, on the elements (u(g) - 1) + a^k v, the value of an equivariant map defined on the projection of Y, and Y is its graph (Artin–Rees).

    Structured proof, 8 steps Lemma 4.1, p. 15 of Columns and rows as Galois representations: labels at the chosen places, anticyclotomic characters at new primes, families of L-values and the crystalline loss at one place

    The Lean statement

  • defRowBlockIndexline 41

    For a type r and a natural number t, RowBlockIndex r : Option (\operatorname{Fin} t) \to Type is the family of index sets of the blocks of a row plus string: it sends none to r and each some e (e < t) to the one-point type Unit.

    For a commutative ring O, a type G, a linearly ordered J, an O-module B, a submodule Z of the functions G \to B, \tau , \varphi : J \times Bool \to G, a finite set I \subseteq J, C \in O and a bound b: HasTriangleCost Z \tau \varphi I C b holds when there is L \subseteq G such that (1) for every j \in I and every c \in Z vanishing on L: if for all i < j, c(\tau(i, true)) = c(\tau(i, false)) and (i \in I \Rightarrow c(\varphi(i, true)) = c(\varphi(i, false))), then C\cdot c(\tau(j, true)) = C\cdot c(\tau(j, false)); and (2) for every submodule S of Z \cap (functions vanishing on L) whose elements vanish at every \tau (k) and satisfy c(\varphi(j, true)) = c(\varphi(j, false)) for j \in I: relLength(Z, functions vanishing on L) + relLength(S, C-torsion) \le b.

  • local_clauses_of_retract unit 77

    Suppose r'(g)P = P·r(φ(g)) for g in a neighbourhood S of 1 (h), P'P = 1 (q), φ an open embedding (he) that is conjugation by c ∈ Aut(Ω) (hφ, hi), and write A' = c⁻¹A (A.comap c, the statement's let). Then (1) if r' is of finite type at A', r is of finite type at A; (2) if moreover PP' = 1, every jump relation JumpsRelAt(r', A', R, J ∘ (· ∘ i)) gives JumpsRelAt(r, A, R, J); (3) for s ∈ S, every polynomial Q killing s on r' at A' kills φ(s) on r at A.   • local_clauses_twist unit 77

    For r' = μ ⊗ r at A: finite type and unramified (crystalline above ℓ) pass from r and μ to r', the jump of μ shifts the jumps, and if X − c kills s on μ and Q(cX) on r then Q kills s on r'.

  • isFiniteTypeAt_char unit 77

    A continuous ℓ-adic character μ with finite image on inertia at A is of finite type at A (potentially crystalline above ℓ), has the single jump 0, and X − μ(s) kills every s. The relation to the literature is the second reader's (literature).

Unit 80 · Galois representations attached to unitary eigenforms and their determinants · 12 declarations

  • Attached unit 80

    Attached(P, ρ, j) takes a family of complex polynomials P_w indexed by the finite places of the CM field E, a representation ρ of a group H with a topology on an n-indexed vector space over the algebraic closure of ℚ_ℓ, and a map j from H to Gal(Ω/E). For H = Gal(Ω/E), j defaults to the identity. The representation is continuous, the trace pairing (X,Y) ↦ tr(XY) is nondegenerate on the linear span of ρ(H), and j is open. For every real η > 0, for most geometric Frobenius data (w,A,s), for every x in H with j(x) = s and every index i, the ℓ-adic distance between coefficient i of the characteristic polynomial of ρ(x) and coefficient i of ι_ℓ(P_w) is at most η. Here most means that each nonempty open subset of Gal(Ω/E) contains a nonempty open subset O such that, outside finitely many w, every Frobenius datum at w with s in O satisfies this condition.

  • GalRep unit 80

    For a prime ℓ, a field E embedded in Ω and a finite type m: the homomorphisms Gal(Ω/E) → GL_m(ℚ̄_ℓ); continuity is not part of the definition.

  • HasJumpsAt unit 80

    For each embedding inducing A above ℓ and each compatible τ : E → ℚ̄_ℓ, the Hodge–Tate jumps of r, with multiplicity, are J(τ) (τ read as a complex embedding through ιℓ).

  • IsFiniteTypeAt unit 80

    r is trivial on the inertia elements near 1 at A if ℓ is a unit at A, and potentially crystalline along every embedding into ℚ̄_ℓ inducing A.

  • IsGoodAt unit 80

    r is trivial on the inertia group of A if ℓ is a unit at A, and crystalline along every embedding into ℚ̄_ℓ inducing A.

  • PadicPlaceEmbedding unit 80

    For a number field E embedded in Ω, a place A of Ω and a prime ℓ: an embedding e : Ω → ℚ̄_ℓ inducing A together with an open embedding toGp of the decomposition group of A into G_ℓ = Gal(ℚ̄_ℓ/ℚ_ℓ), compatible with e (InducesPlace).

  • PadicPlaceEmbedding.isWeilPair_zero_iff unit 80

    (toGp s, 0) is a Weil pair iff s is in the inertia group of A.

  • PolyKillsAt unit 80

    For a representation r, a place A of Ω, s and a polynomial P: P(r(s)) = 0 when ℓ is a unit at A, and IsKilledBy holds for every Weil pair (s, k) along every embedding in ℚ̄_ℓ inducing A.

  • avatarCompatAt_of_stablyUnram unit 80

    For a stably unramified CM field E, the ℓ-adic avatar of an algebraic character θ satisfies the local clauses AvatarCompatAt at every place A above every finite place w: finite type, jumps, and, for s with s⁻¹ a Frobenius of degree m ≤ 1, s = Art(x) with ord x = m and X − ιℓ(θ_w(x)) killing s. The relation to the literature is the second reader's (literature).

  • eventually_notMem_star_notMem unit 80

    For ℓ ≠ 0 and a finite set S of places: for all but finitely many w, w ∉ S, w̄ ∉ S and w is not above ℓ.

  • isRoot_of_isRoot_charpoly unit 80

    If P(M) = 0, every eigenvalue of M is a root of P.

  • sphericalCompat_std unit 80

    Every eigenform on the definite U(n) has an attached ℓ-adic representation; where K_w fixes f, each attached one is unramified or crystalline at w with the jumps of the weight, and the Hecke polynomial kills geometric Frobenius. The relation to the literature is the second reader's (literature).

Unit 102 · Adelic cusp forms, algebraic modular forms and the Petersson pairing · 43 declarations

  • BaseLevel unit 102

    Base data of forms of weight κ on a definite space V with bound m: a finite set S of places, a space Y of forms, stable under every operator commuting with the translations by K₀ at S, contained in a level space of an open level for the translations at S, with |Γ ∩ gK₀g⁻¹| dividing m for all g.

  • BaseLevel.Eigensystem unit 102

    Systems of eigenvalues of level L: the isotypic spaces that contain a nonzero form of formsOfLevel L (with the attached Galois representation galRep).

  • BaseLevel.ellIntegralForms unit 102

    The forms whose values at all points integral above ℓ have coordinates that ιℓ sends to ℓ-adic integers.

  • BaseLevel.formsOfLevel unit 102

    The forms in Y fixed by the components off S ∪ S̄ of the principal level subgroup of K₀ at the places L.

  • EllAdicIntegers unit 102

  • Isotypic unit 102

    Isotypic spaces: for a system t of eigenvalues at all places, the space of forms that are spherical eigenforms with eigenvalues t_w at almost all split w.

  • Isotypic.attached_galRep unit 102

  • Isotypic.isEigenform unit 102

  • LevelPlaces unit 102

  • LevelPlaces.LevelForm.eq_zero_of_innerAt_self unit 102

    For K ⊆ L ⊆ Ω with L/K finite, a homomorphism Γ' → Γ of their Galois groups compatible with the actions on Ω is an open embedding.

  • isOpen_ker_cycCharMod unit 92

    The kernel of cycCharMod Ω N is open in the profinite Galois group.

  • map_cycChar_smul unit 92

    For Ω algebraically closed of characteristic 0, a ℤ_l-module A and an additive map f : A → B whose values are killed by l^r: f(cycChar Ω l (σ)·x) = cycCharMod Ω (l^r) σ·f(x).

  • sah unit 92

    Sah's lemma: let x : G → M satisfy x(g·k) = x(g) + a(g)·x(k) with scalars a(g) in R. If x vanishes at (γ·g)⁻¹·(g·γ), then (a(γ) − 1)·x(g) = (a(g) − 1)·x(γ).

  • valuation_comap_intQpBar_lt_one unit 92

    For a ring homomorphism ι from a field into ℚ̄_p, p has valuation < 1 at the valuation subring that is the preimage under ι of the integers of ℚ̄_p.

    A choice, for each infinite place w of a field E, of a complex embedding Φ(w) of E that induces w (the CM type, when E is CM).

  • ResField unit 123

    For a finite place w of E: the residue field of the completion E_w.

  • Set.conjClosure unit 123

    For a set S of finite places: S ∪ S̄, the places of S and their complex conjugates.

  • StablyUnram unit 123

    A CM field is stably unramified when it contains a primitive root of unity of order 5040 = 7!.

  • algebraMap_ResField_eq_zero unit 123

    An integer maps to 0 in the residue field of w iff it lies in w.

  • atSplit_mem_supportedOn unit 123

    An element placed at a split place w is supported at {w}.

  • card_ResField unit 123

  • residue_eq_iff_valuation_sub_lt_one unit 123

  • uniformizer unit 123

  • v_algebraMap_eq_valuation unit 123

  • Φ_eq_or unit 123

  • FinPlace.below unit 123

  • Nat.Coprime.valuation_eq_one unit 123

  • StablyUnram.exists_isUnit_sub_star unit 123

  • finite_natCast_mem unit 123

  • trivialHecke unit 123

Unit 3 · Density of degree-one Frobenius elements · 8 declarations

  • FrobeniusDatum unit 3

    For a number field E embedded in Ω: a triple (w, A, s) of a finite place w of prime norm, a place A of Ω above w and s ∈ Gal(Ω/E) such that s⁻¹ is an arithmetic Frobenius at A (s is a geometric Frobenius).

  • chebotarev unit 3

    Chebotarev, existence form: for a nonempty open W ⊆ Gal(Ω/K) and a finite set S of primes there is v ∉ S of prime norm with a place A above it, an arithmetic Frobenius σ ∈ W at A, and every arithmetic Frobenius at A in W. The relation to the literature is the second reader's (literature).

  • dense_of_mostFrobenii unit 3

    If p holds at the Frobenius element of most Frobenius data, the set where p holds is dense.

  • eventually_mostFrobenii unit 3

    If p holds for all but finitely many places w and p(x.w) implies q(x) for every Frobenius datum x, then q holds for most Frobenii.

  • mostFrobenii unit 3

    The filter 'most Frobenii' on Frobenius data: a set belongs to it when every nonempty open set of Gal(Ω/E) has a nonempty open part all of whose geometric Frobenii, at almost all places of prime norm, lie in the set.

  • mostFrobenii_push unit 3

    Along an open embedding φ and a map π of places: a property of most Frobenii of E holds for most Frobenii of F at the corresponding data.

  • tendsto_mostFrobeniiAt unit 3

  • mostFrobeniiAt unit 3

Unit 57 · Fitting ideal bounds and multivariate Hensel lifting · 36 declarations

  • fittingIdeal unit 57

    Fitt(N + I·R^ι) ⊆ Fitt(N) + I.

  • fittingIdeal.triangle unit 57

  • relLength_mono unit 57

    Let E be a CM field with Weil data and O an auxiliary pair over E (W of rank r, V = I_n). From Lean's own library · 210 facts

  • Algebra.IsAlgebraic

    An algebra is algebraic when every element is a root of a nonzero polynomial over the base.

  • Finset.prod_dvd_of_coprime

    A product of pairwise coprime divisors of a polynomial divides it.

  • Finset.sum_biUnion

    A sum over a union of pairwise disjoint finite sets is the sum of the sums over the sets.

  • Finsupp.linearCombination

    For a family v : α → M of vectors, the R-linear map (α →₀ R) → M sending a finitely supported θ to Σ_a θ(a)·v(a).

  • Ideal.exists_pow_inf_eq_pow_smul

    Artin–Rees: for an ideal I of a Noetherian ring R, a finitely generated R-module M and a submodule N, there is k with I^n M ∩ N = I^{n−k}(I^k M ∩ N) for all n ≥ k.

  • IsAlgClosed.lift

    An algebra map from a subring R into an algebraically closed field extends to any algebraic extension of R (torsion-free target).

  • IsPrimitiveRoot.powerBasis

    A primitive root of unity ζ_N gives a power basis of ℚ(ζ_N) over ℚ.

  • IsPrimitiveRoot.zpow_eq_one_iff_dvd

    For ζ a primitive N-th root of unity, ζ^k = 1 iff N ∣ k.

  • IsUltrametricDist.norm_add_le_max

    Ultrametric inequality: ‖x + y‖ ≤ max(‖x‖, ‖y‖).

  • Matrix.card_GL_field

    The order of GL_n(𝔽_q) is ∏_{k<n} (q^n − q^k).

  • Matrix.charpoly_inv

    The characteristic polynomial of an invertible matrix A⁻¹ is the reversed characteristic polynomial of A divided by its constant term.

Where it is used

Unit 137 · A Taylor–Wiles prime at a time: the deformed trace at one auxiliary place clears the denominator, Unit 139 · At most one primitive label per Hecke eigensystem at a chosen place (via Galois representations of lifts), Unit 140 · Good idempotents, labels and the transfer of traces between unitary groups, Unit 143 · The rows: target forms on an indefinite U(r), the volume of the chain of lifts as a product of lift volumes, and the height of each lift volume, Unit 147 · The exchange identity: theta integrals of complementary rank agree up to a constant, and cruder forms complete a family to a basis in the lower bound, Unit 151 · The endgame: the weighted prime sum and its remainder, the one-sided criterion (Landau), the choice of the second scale, and the statements and assembly of Theorems A, B and C
